CANTONMENT BOARD. BABINA CANTONMENT 
Babina Cautt, the 22nd June 1985 

S.R.O. CRI Bye-Laws.—Whereas a draft of certain bye-laws 
further to amend the Babina Cantonment (Regulation and 
Control of Plying and Licensing Rickshaws) Bye-Laws 1978, 
published with the Notification of tlie Government of India 
in the Ministry of Defence, No. S.R.O. CR|Bye-Lawa|78, 
dated 10th February, 1978 was published with the Canton¬ 
ment Board’s puhlic Notice No. CR| Bye-Law? 11871, dated 
the 8th November, 1982, as required by sub-section (1) of 
Section 284 of the Cantonments Act, 1924 (2 of 1924) for 
inviting objections and suggestions from all persons likely to 
bo affected thereby till the expiry of thirty days from the 
date of publication of the said notice : 

And whereas the said notice was put on the Cantonment 
Notice Boaird on 8th November 1982; 

And whereas no objection or sugge,stion was received from 
tha nubile by the Cantonment Board, Babina before the said 
period: 

And whereas the Central Government have duly approved 
and confirmed the said draft of the Bye-Laws as required by 
sub-section (1) of section 284 of the Cantonments Act, 1924 
(2 of 1924); 


Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-setion (25), (26), and (27) of section 282 of the 
Cantonments Act, 1924 (2 of 1924), the Cantonment Board, 
Babina, hereby makes the following bye-Iaws namely :— 

1. Short title and Commencement — 

(1) These Bye-laws may be called the Babina Cantonment 
(Regulation and control of Plying and Licenssing Rickshaws) 
Arnondment Bye-Laws, 1982. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of tholr publica¬ 
tion in the Official Gazette. 

2. In byo.kvvs 5 of the Babina Contonraent (Regulation 
and Control of Plying and Licensing Rickshaws) Bye-Laws, 
1978, for “Rates of fee" and entries relating thereto, the 
following shall be substituted namely 

“Rates of fee 

(i) For a Rickshaw pHying for hire. 

For 12 months . Rs. 24|- 

(ii) for a rickshaw plying for private use, 

fca- 12 months.Rs. 201-."
